loo","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-22T21:51:11+00:00","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2604_21106.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答一个很具体但一直缺少定量答案的问题：在 looped / depth-recurrent language model 里，多一次 recurrence 到底相当于增加了多少“等效独立参数”。过去大家知道参数共享会带来容量损失，也知道循环深度可能带来某种推理归纳偏置，但没有一个把 recurrence 直接映射到参数等价物的标尺。论文通过 iso-depth、matched-FLOPs 的预训练扫点，把“共享一层反复用”和“增加新的独立层”之间的差距显式量化出来。","importance":"对预训练研究，这篇工作的价值主要在方法论而不是新架构本身。它给 looped LM 提供了一个可比较的 scaling-law 坐标系：不是只看固定参数下谁更省显存，也不是只看固定 compute 下谁 loss 更低，而是用一个 recurrence-equivalence exponent φ 来衡量参数共享的真实代价。这个量对后续任何 looped 架构、训练 recipe、甚至带 recurrence 的 reasoning-oriented 设计都很有用，因为它把讨论从“感觉上更深”变成“每次循环值多少独立容量”。论文的结论也比较克制：在他们的 prelude-recur-coda 设定下，额外 recurrence 远不是 1:1 替代独立层，φ=0.46 意味着共享块重复执行带来的有效容量增长明显次线性。对想把 recurrence 当成便宜深度扩展手段的人，这个结果很关键。","technical_approach":"技术路线分两层。第一层是实验设计。作者构造四种 prelude-recur-coda 变体，r∈{1,2,4,8}，其中 r=1 是非循环基线。它们都保持 20 层 effective depth；具体做法是固定 prelude 和 coda 各 2 层，把中间 recurrent block 的独立层数设为 16/r，再执行 r 次。这样在相同宽度下，各模型每 token 的训练和推理 FLOPs 基本一致，effective depth 也一致，唯一系统变化的是参数共享程度。这个设计比“固定参数、增加 recurrence”更干净，因为后者会同时改变深度、共享率和推理成本。第二层是 joint scaling law。作者没有分别给每个 r 拟合一条独立 Chinchilla 曲线后再做松散比较，而是提出统一形式 L = E + A (N_once + r^φ N_rec)^(-α) + B D^(-β)。这里 N_once 是只执行一次的 prelude+coda 参数，N_rec 是共享 recurrent block 的独立参数量，φ 则表示 recurrence 的参数等价指数。φ=1 表示每多一次循环都等价于增加同量独立参数，没有共享损失；φ=0 表示重复执行完全不增加有效容量，只是同一块反复算。这个参数化把“共享块到底值多少”直接嵌进 scaling law 里，并允许用所有 116 个训练点联合拟合。论文还分别做了 per-architecture Chinchilla fit，用来观察不同 r 下 compute-optimal 宽度和 token 分配如何变化。结果显示 recurrence 越高，最优模型倾向更宽、每参数训练 token 更少；但因为更宽会抬高单 token compute，在固定总 compute 下总训练 token 反而更少。","experiments":"实验规模是 116 次预训练，覆盖 r∈{1,2,4,8} 和 6 个 compute budget，从 4.64×10^17 到 2.15×10^19 FLOPs，跨度约 50 倍。核心结果是 joint scaling law 拟合得到 φ=0.46，R²=0.997。这个数字给出很直观的解释：额外 recurrence 有效，但远低于线性等价。文中举的例子是，在 r=4 且训练 FLOPs 匹配时，looped 模型只能达到比非循环模型少 42% 参数时的验证损失水平。更具体地说，一个 410M 的 r=4 模型，表现大致相当于 580M 的非循环模型，但训练成本接近一个 1B 非循环模型，因为两者 per-token FLOPs 匹配而 looped 最优点需要更宽。也就是说，参数内存省下来了，但 compute 并没有换来同等容量。下游评测方面，作者做了五个轴的 suite。结论是 parametric-knowledge 类任务能较直接反映验证损失差距；open-book 任务随着 validation loss 降低会逐渐缩小差距；reasoning 相关轴在当前 development-scale compute 下信号接近噪声，尚不足以支持“looping 自带明显推理优势”的强结论。这个结果很重要，因为它提醒大家不要把 recurrence 的潜在 reasoning bias 过早当成已证实收益。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先抓三处。第一是 Methodology 里架构控制变量的设计，尤其为什么要做 iso-depth、matched per-token FLOPs，而不是更常见的 fixed-parameter sweep。这里决定了 φ 的解释是否干净。第二是 joint scaling law 的定义与拟合结果，重点看 φ 的物理含义、参考端点 φ=0 和 φ=1，以及 Figure 1 上 free-φ 拟合与 φ=1 假设之间的偏离。第三是 per-architecture Chinchilla fit 和 compute-optimal allocation 的变化，这部分对真正要训练 looped LM 的人更实用。若只花 25–35 分钟，读 Introduction、Methodology、joint fit 结果图表和 downstream evaluation 结论即可。更值得深挖的点有三个：一是 φ 是否会随训练 recipe、optimizer、位置编码或 input injection 设计变化；二是这个指数是否依赖架构族，prelude-recur-coda 之外未必成立；三是如果把 recurrence 与更强的中间状态约束、adaptive compute 或 latent reasoning 结合，能否把 φ 往上推，而不是只在现有 recipe 下接受 0.46 这个值。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a precise question that looped language models have lacked a clean answer to: how much is one additional recurrence worth in terms of equivalent unique parameters? Prior work established that parameter sharing reduces memory and may induce useful depth-related behavior, but it did not quantify how repeated application of a shared block compares to adding new non-shared layers under controlled compute.","importance":"Its main contribution is methodological. It gives looped LMs a scaling-law lens with a directly interpretable quantity, the recurrence-equivalence exponent φ. That is more useful than comparing models only at fixed parameter count or fixed compute, because it isolates the actual cost of parameter sharing. For anyone studying recurrent depth, parameter-efficient pretraining, or reasoning-oriented architectures with shared blocks, this provides a benchmark: not whether looping feels deeper, but how much effective capacity each recurrence really buys. The conclusion is restrained but important: in the tested prelude-recur-coda setup, extra recurrence is far from a one-for-one substitute for unique depth. Transforme","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-23T03:46:14+00:00","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2604_21254.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体、也很现实的问题：在总算力和延迟大致受限时，怎样把语言模型的参数占用继续压下去，同时不把困惑度做坏。普通 Transformer 的深度扩展依赖逐层新增参数，适合云端但不适合内存紧张的端侧。已有 looped / recurrent-depth Transformer 通过跨深度共享参数来省参数，但常见问题是：同等展开深度下，参数省了，perplexity 往往比普通 Transformer 更差。作者要解决的不是“能不能做循环深度”，而是“能不能把循环深度做成真正更优的性能-参数折中”，尤其是在 240M、1B、2B 这种已经接近实用的规模上。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要有两层。第一，它给“参数效率”这个目标提供了一个比单纯缩小模型更像架构改造的答案：不是减少训练深度，而是保留足够的计算深度，把中间块反复应用，只为这部分深度付一次参数成本。第二，它说明 looped Transformer 过去的主要短板并不一定来自“共享参数”本身，而更可能来自共享后表示流动能力不足、重复迭代时信息混合不够。作者用 hyper-connections 只加在 loop 级别，而不是每层都加，说明这类结构增强可以很便宜地修补共享深度的表达瓶颈。如果结论稳固，这对边端模型、低内存部署、以及未来超大模型的参数驻留问题都有启发。不过它更像一条清晰的架构方向，而不是会立刻改写主流大模型训练范式的工作，所以放在 skim 是合适的。","technical_approach":"方法上有两个关键设计。第一是 middle-cycle 架构：把网络分成 begin、middle、end 三段，只有 middle block 被循环执行，多次展开形成有效深度。这样做比“全模型所有层都共享”更稳，因为前后两端仍保留非共享层，分别负责输入表征塑形和最终输出整合，减轻完全共享带来的容量损失。第二是在 looped middle block 上引入 hyper-connections，但作者没有采用标准的 layer-level hyper-connections，而是改成 loop-level hyper-connections。也就是，一个 middle block 内部仍是普通多层 Transformer；真正的多流 residual 扩展、读写和混合，只发生在每次 loop 迭代之间。\n\n这个改动很关键。若在每层都做 hyper-connections，参数和计算都会明显上升，抵消 looped 架构节省参数的意义。作者把它提升到“每次循环后再混合”的粒度，相当于把多流残差当成跨迭代状态，而不是层内状态。这样做的直觉是：循环深度的主要问题不在单次 block 内部表达不够，而在多次重复应用同一组参数时，残差信息容易塌缩到单一路径，导致后续迭代越来越像重复加工同一表示。loop-level hyper-connections 给了模型一个更丰富的跨迭代记忆与路由机制，让不同 residual stream 在每次循环后重新组合，缓解共享参数导致的表示僵化。\n\n从全文描述看，作者采用的是 manifold-constrained hyper-connections 一脉的形式：把原始 C 维 residual 扩展成 n×C 的多流表示，并通过输入相关的 pre/post/res 投影进行读取、写入和残差混合。但他们只在 loop 边界使用这些投影，因此额外参数和 FLOPs 都较小。换句话说，这篇工作的创新不在发明全新的 hyper-connection 数学形式，而在于找到一个对 looped Transformer 最合适、成本最低的插入位置。","experiments":"实验结论很直接：Hyperloop Transformer 在 240M、1B、2B 三个规模上，都能在约一半参数量下取得比 depth-matched 普通 Transformer 更低的 perplexity。这里最重要的不是“比某个弱 loop baseline 好”，而是它能超过普通非共享 Transformer，这一点决定了它是否只是参数压缩技巧，还是一个真正改善性能-内存前沿的架构。\n\n摘要里给出的核心数字是“约 50% fewer parameters”，同时在这些规模上 perplexity 仍优于 depth-matched baselines。全文引言还强调，这种优势在 post-training quantization 后依然存在，说明收益不是依赖高精度权重存储的脆弱现象，而是结构本身在量化后仍保留较好的性能-内存折中。对端侧部署来说，这一点比单纯 FP16 下的困惑度更重要。\n\n从论文结构推断，值得关注的消融应包括几类：一是 middle-cycle 相比 fully looped 或普通 Transformer 的差异；二是 hyper-connections 放在 loop-level 与 layer-level、或完全不加时的差异；三是不同 expansion factor n、不同循环次数、不同 begin/middle/end 深度配比的影响。如果这些消融显示收益主要来自 loop-level mixing，而不是简单增加一点参数，那就支持作者的核心论点：共享深度的瓶颈是跨迭代表达流动，而不是纯容量不足。\n\n需要注意的是，当前给出的结果主要围绕 perplexity 和量化后的性能-参数前沿。若全文没有覆盖长上下文、推理任务、训练稳定性、吞吐和 wall-clock 成本，那么这篇工作的结论仍应理解为“适合内存受限 LM 预训练的架构候选”，而不是已经完成全面替代普通 Transformer 的验证。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓三件事。第一，看方法图和架构定义，确认 begin/middle/end 的具体层数分配，以及 hyper-connections 究竟插在 block 内还是 block 间。第二，看主结果表，重点不是绝对 perplexity，而是同等展开深度、相近训练预算下，参数量减少多少、困惑度改善多少。第三，看消融，尤其是 loop-level hyper-connections 是否比 layer-level 更划算。\n\n如果时间有限，建议优先读 Introduction、方法总览图、主实验表、量化实验，以及关于 hyper-connections 插入位置的消融，预计 25-35 分钟能抓住主要信息。若要进一步判断它对预训练架构研究有没有持续价值，再细看附录里的参数/FLOPs 统计、训练设定、以及不同 loop 次数下的 scaling 行为。真正值得深挖的点有两个：一是共享参数模型的性能损失到底来自哪里；二是多流 residual 是否可以视为一种廉价的“跨迭代状态空间”，这可能比这篇论文本身更有延展性。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a concrete architecture question: how to reduce model memory footprint without giving up the effective depth and language modeling quality of a Transformer. Standard Transformers pay parameter cost layer by layer. That is acceptable in cloud settings, but much less so for edge or on-device deployment. Looped / recurrent-depth Transformers reuse parameters across depth and are therefore attractive for memory-constrained inference, yet they usually lose on perplexity when compared against depth-matched non-shared baselines. The real problem here is not whether parameter sharing works at all, but whether a looped architecture can actually improve the performance-memory frontier rather than merely trading quality for fewer weights.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is fairly specific. First, it treats parameter efficiency as an architectural objective, not just a smaller-model objective. The model keeps substantial compute depth by repeatedly applying a middle block, while paying the parameter cost only once for that block. Second, it suggests that the main weakness of looped Transformers may not be parameter sharing per se, but insufficient information routing across repeated applications of the same block. The proposed loop-level hyper-connections are a lightweight way to restore representational flexibility. If that interpretation holds, the paper matters for edge LMs, low-memory deployment, and possibly future frontier-scale training where parameter residency itself becomes a systems bottleneck. This is still an architectural direction rather than a broad paradigm shift, so skim is the right level.","technical_approach":"$1c","experiments":"$1d","reading_guide":"$1e","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"三段式循环加超连接的架构设计对小参数/端侧LLM研发有参考价值，重点看§3的架构实现细节","en":"The three-stage looped Transformer with hyper-connections design is informative for small/on-device LLM R&D, focus on implementation details in Section 3"},"metadata":{"institutions":["Massachusetts Institute of Technology"],"authors":["Abbas Zeitoun","Lucas Torroba-Hennigen","Yoon Kim"],"notable_authors":["Yoon Kim"],"tags":["LLM Architecture","Parameter Efficient","Looped Transformer","On-device LLM"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21254","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.21254","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2604.21330","title":"Teacher-Guided Routing for Sparse Vision Mixture-of-Experts","abstract":"Recent progress in deep learning has been driven by increasingly large-scale models, but the resulting computational cost has become a critical bottleneck. 很快收益递减。论文要解决的核心问题，是如何在不做复杂搜索或额外优化的前提下，同时自适应地分配测试时算力，并让生成分布随着测试过程演化。","importance":"它对预训练本身不是直接贡献，更接近 post-train / inference-time reasoning 方法。但对基座研究仍有两点启发。第一，它把 test-time compute 的收益拆成两个来源：算力分配和分布重塑，这个拆分很清楚，也有助于理解为什么单纯 best-of-N 会很快饱和。第二，它展示了一个轻量级的 inference loop：利用测试集自身已经解决的问题构造 evolving in-context demonstrations，逐步改变后续题目的条件分布。这对研究 ICL 机制、推理时自举、以及 pretrain 与 in-context demonstration pool。第二阶段是 adaptive phase：只把后续预算投给尚未解决的问题，但关键不只是继续采样，而是先从 demonstration pool 中按语义相似度检索若干已成功样例，把这些样例作为 ICL demonstrations 拼到当前问题前面，再在这个新条件分布下生成。随着更多问题被解决，pool 持续增长，后续题目的生成分布也持续变化。这样，compute allocation 和 distribution adaptation 被放进同一个闭环。和已有方法相比，它不需要为每个 query 单独做搜索，也不依赖额外训练 verifier 或 policy；和纯 difficulty-based allocation 相比，它不是只决定 sample 数，而是试图提高每次 sample 的命中率。论文里还把问题形式化为 coverage 优化：只要某题生成集合里至少有一个正确答案，就算该题被解决，因此目标是用有限预算最大化被覆盖的问题数。","experiments":"作者在 MATH-500、GPQA-Diamond、LiveCodeBench、MinervaMath 上做实验，比较对象包括 uniform allocation baseline 和只按难度分配预算的 adaptive allocation baseline。文中强调两点结果：一是在多个随机种子和多轮评测下，方法都能在更低 compute budget 下取得更高性能；二是收益主要出现在 test-time scaling 的早期阶段，说明改进来自更有效的预算使用和更好的条件分布，而不是后期暴力堆 sample。由于给出的全文片段没有完整结果表，无法准确摘出提升百分比、预算大小、每题 sample 上限、检索 demonstration 数量等关键数字。阅读时需要重点确认三类消融：只做 allocation、不做 evolving ICL；只做 ICL、不做 allocation；以及 demonstration 检索策略和 pool 质量对结果的影响。如果这些消融充分，论文的主要价值就在于证明“动态演化的 ICL 上下文”确实能改变 test-time scaling 的收益曲线。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先看 Problem Setting 和 Methods。前者把 coverage 目标和 sequential allocation 形式化得比较清楚，能快速看出它与 best-of-N 的区别。后者重点看 warm-up pool、语义检索、以及 evolving demonstrations 如何嵌入生成流程。实验部分不必逐题细看，先抓预算-性能曲线，确认收益是否稳定出现在低预算区间，再看消融判断改进来自哪里。预计 20-30 分钟可完成泛读。最值得留意的风险点有三个：一是测试集内样本互相当 demonstration，是否会引入 benchmark-specific leakage 风险；二是语义相似检索在数学、代码、科学问答上是否真的对应可迁移解法；三是随着 pool 扩大，错误示例是否会污染后续生成分布。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific limitation in test-time scaling. Existing methods usually decide where to spend extra inference compute, but they keep sampling from essentially the same conditional distribution for each query. Even adaptive allocation methods often only vary the number of samples per question while leaving p(y|x) unchanged. That leads to diminishing returns on hard problems when correct solutions have low probability under the base model. The core problem here is how to adapt both compute allocation and the generation distribution itself during inference, without expensive per-query search or additional optimization.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense; it sits closer to post-training and inference-time reasoning. Still, it has two useful implications for foundation-model research. First, it cleanly separates the gains from test-time compute into allocation effects and distribution-shaping effects, which helps explain why plain best-of-N saturates quickly. Second, it offers a lightweight self-bootstrapping inference loop where solved test examples become evolving in-context demonstrations for unsolved ones. That is relevant to researchers interested in in-context learning dynamics, self-adaptation at inference time, and the interface between pretrained priors and test-time conditioning. The contribution is practical rather than paradigm-shifting.","technical_approach":"$1f","experiments":"$20","reading_guide":"For a skim, start with the Problem Setting and Methods sections. We present SparKV, an adaptive KV loading framework that combines cloud-based KV streaming with on-device computation. SparKV models the cost of individual KV chunks and decides whether each chunk should be streamed or computed locally, while overlapping the two execu","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-23T02:55:31+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2604_21231.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.NI","cs.AI","cs.PF"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是端侧 LLM 推理里的 prefill 延迟问题，具体是“已有上下文可复用时，KV cache 到底该从云端传，还是在本地重新算”。现有方案通常只押注一边：要么做 KV 压缩后传输，要么优化本地 prefill 计算。但在真实边缘设备上，通信带宽、算力余量、内存带宽都在波动，而且不同 KV chunk 的传输成本和重算成本差异很大。论文要解决的核心问题，是在严格的 Transformer 依赖约束下，对每个 chunk 做 overhead-aware 的路径选择，并让云传输与本地计算尽可能重叠，从而降低 TTFT。","importance":"对基座预训练研究的直接相关性不高，它更偏 serving / efficient inference，而不是建模或训练方法。但它有两个可借鉴点。第一，它把 KV cache 看成可按 token、head、layer 多维切分并调度的对象，这种更细粒度的 cache 抽象，对长上下文 serving 和训练时 activation / state 管理都有启发。第二，它强调“不要盲目 overlap，而要按 chunk 成本路由”，这是一种很工程但很扎实的系统思路：瓶颈不是单一算力或单一带宽，而是依赖图上的异构代价最优化。若你的关注点是推理系统、边缘部署、context reuse，这篇有实际价值；若只看 pretrain，可快速略读。","technical_approach":"SparKV 的方法由三部分组成。第一是 KV chunk scheduler。云端先把预计算好的 KV cache 按 token、attention head、Transformer layer 进行索引化切分。调度器随后对每个 chunk 决定走哪条路径：直接从云端流式加载，还是在设备端根据原始上下文局部重算。这个决策不是静态规则，而是要满足层间和因果依赖，同时最小化端到端 TTFT。\n\n第二是 overhead model。作者用一个轻量 MLP 预测每个 chunk 的本地计算时延，输入特征来自 attention sparsity 等信息。这样做的原因是，不同 chunk 的重算代价并不均匀：有些 chunk 因为稀疏注意力或结构位置原因，本地算更便宜；另一些则更适合直接传。相比统一按层或按段切分，这种 per-chunk 成本建模更细。\n\n第三是 runtime controller。离线生成的调度在真实无线网络和边缘设备上不够稳，因为吞吐和可用算力会波动。控制器在滑动窗口里监控无线带宽和设备 compute headroom，运行时动态把部分 chunk 从“传输路径”迁到“本地计算路径”，或反过来迁移，以重新平衡通信与计算。论文的关键主张不是简单的 cloud-edge 协同，而是依赖感知、成本感知、并且能在线修正的协同。\n\n与已有工作相比，区别主要有三点：1）不是单纯做 KV 压缩或优先级传输；2）不是只优化本地 prefill kernel/sparse attention；3）不是粗粒度的双向并行，而是更细粒度地在 chunk 级别做路由和 overlap。作者认为，只有这样才能在严格依赖约束下真正吃到通信和计算的并行收益。","experiments":"实验覆盖多个 LLM、数据集和边缘设备，主指标是 TTFT，同时也看能耗和输出质量保持。引言里给出的核心结果是：相对已有高效 KV loading 方案，SparKV 将 TTFT 降低到 1.3× 到 5.1× 的加速区间，单请求能耗降低 1.5× 到 3.3×，并且在真实无线环境和算力波动下仍保持鲁棒。文中还给了一个很直观的背景数字：在 Redmi K80 Pro 16GB 上，顺序 prefill 处理 15K 以上上下文会超过 15 秒；即便在 Jetson Orin 上使用稀疏注意力，15K context 仍需 5 秒以上，这说明问题设定并不轻。\n\n从论文结构推测，关键消融应包括：是否使用 overhead-aware 调度、是否启用 runtime controller、chunk 切分粒度变化、以及不同网络条件下的性能退化。真正值得看的不是平均加速比，而是它在带宽抖动和设备负载变化时是否还能维持稳定收益。如果主表之外还有 schedule 可视化或 chunk 路由分布图，那会帮助判断该方法是否真的学到了“哪些块该传、哪些块该算”，而不是仅仅利用某个固定场景。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合系统向泛读。先看问题设定和 Figure 1，确认三类 workflow 的差别：纯传输、纯本地算、以及 SparKV 的混合路径。然后看 scheduler 的依赖建模，重点不是公式细节，而是它如何在 token/head/layer 三个维度切 chunk，同时不破坏 Transformer 的执行依赖。接着看 overhead model 的输入特征和预测误差，再看 runtime controller 在无线波动下如何改写离线 schedule。最后看真实设备实验和鲁棒性结果。\n\n如果只想抓住方法要点，20–30 分钟足够。若你做 serving，可以多看调度算法和 runtime adaptation；若你做 pretrain，这篇主要看它对“状态缓存可被细粒度调度”的系统抽象，不必深挖实现细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses prefill latency for on-device LLM inference, specifically the decision problem of whether reusable KV cache should be streamed from the cloud or recomputed locally. Existing systems usually commit to one side: either compress and transmit KV caches, or accelerate local prefill. In practice, edge devices face fluctuating bandwidth, limited compute headroom, and heterogeneous chunk costs. Different KV chunks have very different communication and recomputation overheads. The core problem here is therefore to make per-chunk routing decisions under strict Transformer dependencies, while overlapping cloud streaming and local computation as much as possible to reduce time-to-first-token.","importance":"This is not directly a pretraining paper; it sits in efficient inference / serving. Still, it offers two useful ideas. First, it treats KV cache as a schedulable object that can be partitioned across token, head, and layer dimensions, which is a more fine-grained systems abstraction than many prior works. That abstraction may be relevant to long-context serving and, more loosely, to thinking about state management in training systems. Second, the paper’s main lesson is practical and sound: overlap is not enough by itself; routing must be cost-aware. The bottleneck is not just compute or just bandwidth, but optimization over a dependency graph with heterogeneous costs. For researchers focused on edge deployment or context reuse, this is useful. For pure pretraining work, it is peripheral.","technical_approach":"$21","experiments":"$22","reading_guide":"This is best read as a systems paper. a","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-22T04:22:32+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2604_20920.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"如何在不改 signal），从而把稀疏注意力的选择机制纳入预训练目标里学习，而不是推理期启发式 eviction/selection。它更像一种训练期长上下文稀疏化范式（coarse-to-fine），但论文重心偏高效注意力/长上下文训练，属于 pretrain 相关的系统性改造而非新建模目标。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 Gist Sparse Attention (GSA) 的“压缩→选择→展开”三段式。\n1) Interleaved gist tokens：在序列中按固定间隔插入 gist token，每个 gist 负责汇聚相邻一段 raw tokens（类似局部摘要）。相比 append-at-end 的 gist，这种布局让“gist ↔ chunk”对应关系更明确，便于后续选择与展开。\n2) Compression as routing：先用 gist token 表示整段长上下文的粗粒度视图；对当前 query，基于其对各 gist 的注意力分数做 top-k 选择，选出最相关的若干 gist。\n3) Selective unfolding：把被选 gist 对应的 raw token chunk 重新“展开”回可见上下文，让 query 对这些 chunk 做细粒度注意力；未被选中的 chunk 继续保持压缩态，避免全量二次方交互。\n4) 训练方式：把上述机制放进训练期，使模型学会产生“既能压缩信息、又能作为路由信号”的 gist 表示。论文强调不需要像 NSA 那样改注意力结构分支；也不需要像 DSA 那样外置 indexer；相对 MoBA 的 mean-pooling gate，gist 是可学习且可通过梯度优化的路由载体。","experiments":"给出的全文片段未包含完整实验表格与具体数值（如训练 token / gist 插入频率对效果与成本的影响。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"How to unify learnable context compression (gist tokens) with training-time sparse attention routing—without modifying the core Transformer architecture—via a coarse-to-fine pipeline that compresses first, selects relevant summaries, and then selectively restores the corresponding raw chunks for detailed attention.","importance":"The key pretraining-relevant idea is to turn compression tokens into differentiable routing signals learned end-to-end during pretraining, rather than relying on inference-time KV eviction/selection heuristics. Conceptually it bridges “KV compression” and “native sparse attention” by letting the model learn what to compress and how to route attention under a fixed token/compute budget. The contribution is primarily in long-context efficiency/training design, not in a new language modeling objective.","technical_approach":"Gist Sparse Attention (GSA) implements a three-stage coarse-to-fine mechanism:\n1) Interleaved gist tokens: insert gist tokens at regular intervals so each gist corresponds to a local raw-token chunk.\n2) Routing by gist attention: compute attention from the current query to gist tokens and perform top-k selection over gists.\n3) Selective unfolding: re-introduce (unfold) the raw chunks associated with the selected gists, enabling fine-grained attention only over those chunks; the rest stays compressed.\n4) Training-time integration: the whole mechanism is used during training so gist representations become both information-dense summaries and learnable gates. The paper positions this against NSA (architecture changes), DSA (external indexer), and MoBA (non-differentiable mean-pooling gates).","experiments":"The provided full-text excerpt does not include the experiment tables/metrics, so specific numbers (context length, compute savings, perplexity/accuracy deltas) cannot be verified here.\nFrom the introduction-level claims: under the same token budget, selective unfolding improves accuracy over pure gist compression baselines (append-only and interleaved) and inference-only sparse attention methods; incorporating it into training yields a unified, end-to-end learnable sparse pattern. For quantitative conclusions, the experimental section is required.","reading_guide":"Skim plan (~25–35 min):\n- Read the Introduction and any method overview figure to understand how GSA relates to NSA/DSA/MoBA and to append-vs-interleaved gist designs.\n- Focus on implementation details: what scores drive top-k selection, where selection happens (prefill vs per-layer), how unfolding respects a fixed token budget, and how training handles the (potentially) non-differentiable selection step.\n- In experiments, extract: (1) quality vs compute at matched budgets, (2) training-time GSA vs inference-only sparsification, and (3) ablations on compression ratio, k, gist frequency, and unfolding on/off.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"可训练的粗细结合长上下文注意力设计，§3的gist路由机制可作为预训练长上下文优化的 ablation 候选","en":"Coarse-to-fine trainable long-context attention design, gist routing mechanism in §3 can be used as ablation candidate for pretraining long-context optimization"},"metadata":{"institutions":["Stanford University"],"authors":["Yuzhen Mao","Michael Y. caption","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-22T09:01:04+00:00","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2604_21718.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI","cs.CL","cs.LG","cs.MM"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是一般的视频 captioning，而是“精确视频语言”如何被系统地构建出来。作者认为现有视频-文本数据集的主要问题不是规模不够，而是标注规范不清、描述粒度不稳定、人工写作成本过高，结果导致模型学到的语言监督噪声很大，容易漏掉时序细节、空间关系和镜头运动，还会产生幻觉。论文把问题拆成三个环节：先定义一套明确的描述规范，再设计可扩展的人机监督流程去生产高质量 caption，最后把监督过程中自然产生的 critique 和 preference 信号用于 post-training。它关心的是如何为视频语言模型建立更精细、更可控的训练语料和训练信号。","importance":"从基座研究角度看，这篇工作的价值主要在数据与监督设计，而不是某个单一模型结构。视频模型长期受限于文本监督过粗：一句“a person walks in a room”对生成和理解都不够，因为它没有把主体、场景、动作、空间构图、镜头语言拆开。作者提出的做法相当于为视频预训练和后训练建立一套更高分辨率的语言接口。对多模态预训练有两点启发。第一，标注规范本身就是训练目标的一部分；如果语言空间定义得含糊，模型学到的视觉概念边界也会含糊。第二，监督流程不必是“人从零写，模型被动学”，也可以是“模型先高召回生成，人类做批判和修订”，再把这个过程沉淀成 preference、critique、revision 三种信号。这个闭环和高质量 post-train 数据引擎很接近，对 VLM、视频生成、甚至通用多模态对齐都有关联。","technical_approach":"方法由三层组成。\n\n第一层是 precise specification。作者与专业视频创作者合作，把视频描述拆成结构化维度，包括 subjects、scenes、motion、spatial framing、camera dynamics 等，并定义数百个视觉与运动 primitive。这里的关键不是列一个标签表，而是把“什么该描述、描述到什么粒度、如何避免歧义”写成可操作的 annotation policy。相比常见开放式 caption，这种规范更接近受控语言：它要求描述可验证、覆盖关键动态因素，并尽量减少不同标注员各写各的自由度。\n\n第二层是 CHAI，Critique-based Human–AI Oversight。流程不是让人类从空白开始写长 caption，而是先由模型生成高召回 pre-caption，再由受训专家进行 critique 和 revision，得到 post-caption。作者强调这种分工的原因：视频细节很多，纯人工写作耗时且容易漏；模型写作流畅但会幻觉；把人类注意力从“生成文本”转到“验证和纠错”更符合认知负担分配。更重要的是，这个流程天然产出三元组：(pre-caption, critique, post-caption)。这比普通 preference pair 更丰富，因为 critique 明确指出差在哪里、如何改。\n\n第三层是 post-training recipe。作者把上述三元组用于 SFT、DPO、以及带视觉奖励或偏好优化的后训练，并研究 inference-time scaling。论文的论点不是“有 preference 就够了”，而是 critique 的质量决定了后训练效果。与一些只收集 winner/loser 对的做法相比，这里多了一条自然语言反馈通道，使模型不仅知道哪个更好，还知道为什么更好、应该补哪些视觉细节、纠正哪些空间或镜头错误。\n\n从方法定位看，这更像一个数据引擎和监督协议，而不是新的视频 backbone。它与预训练的关系在于：作者把语言监督的分辨率、可验证性和可扩展性一起提高了。对多模态基座来说，这往往比局部架构改动更持久。","experiments":"论文给出的数据规模不是超大，而是强调质量和监督密度。作者基于约 4k 视频收集 triplets，覆盖 films、games、commercials、user-generated clips 等多种来源，并发布统一 benchmark 来评估精确视频语言理解。虽然这里提供的全文片段没有完整数表，但从引言能提炼出几类关键实验。\n\n第一类是标注流程对比。作者做了用户研究，比较 CHAI 与纯人工编辑或已有人工修订流程。结论是 CHAI 在 annotation accuracy、writing quality 和效率上都更好。这个结果很关键，因为如果人机协作只是更便宜但质量下降，那对基座训练帮助有限；作者强调的是同时提升质量和效率。\n\n第二类是 post-training 消融。论文指出，仅有 preference 信号不够，成功的 SFT、DPO、RLHF-V 依赖高质量 critique。作者还特别拿 OpenAI GDC 一类 prior critique 数据作比较，认为 CHAI 产生的 critique 更准确、更完整、更具建设性，因此带来的后训练收益更稳定。这个结论对 post-train 数据构造很重要：自然语言反馈不是越多越好，而是要与可观察错误对齐。\n\n第三类是下游效果。作者声称基于这些精细 caption 训练或微调后，视频生成模型在 detailed prompt following、camera dynamics、shot composition、cinematography control 上表现更好。若全文中有 Figure 5 一类可视化，建议重点看，因为这类结果最能说明“精确语言”是否真的转化成更细粒度的控制能力。\n\n第四类是 benchmark 设计。作者发布的 unified benchmark 同时评估多个维度，而不是只看 caption 相似度。这一点很重要，因为传统 caption metric 往往奖励流畅但不够精确的描述，无法衡量左/右、镜头推进、主体进出画面等细节是否正确。\n\n整体看，这篇工作的实验重心不是刷大规模通用分数，而是证明一套监督协议能稳定地产生更高质量的多模态训练信号。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合从“数据与监督系统设计”角度读，而不是当作普通 VLM 模型论文来读。\n\n先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1/2，理解作者为什么把 precise video language 拆成 specification、oversight、post-training 三步。这里决定了整篇工作的边界：它不是单纯做 caption 数据集，而是在定义一种视频语言接口。\n\n然后重点看 structured specification。要关注 primitive 如何被记录和标准化。这里能帮助判断这种监督协议是否可扩展到图像、音频、交互轨迹等别的模态。\n\n实验部分优先看三块：人工评测与效率对比、post-training ablation、benchmark 维度设计。如果时间有限，先跳过具体模型细节，抓住数据生产和监督信号设计即可。预计 35–50 分钟能完成有效泛读；若你关心多模态 post-train 数据引擎，值得再花时间看附录中的标注规范和 critique 示例。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not about generic video captioning. It asks how to build a precise video language that can serve as reliable supervision for video-language models. The authors argue that current video-text datasets are limited less by size than by weak annotation policy, inconsistent granularity, and the high cognitive cost of writing detailed captions from scratch. As a result, models trained on such data miss temporal, spatial, and camera-motion details and often hallucinate. The paper breaks the problem into three parts: define a clear specification of what should be described, build a scalable human-AI oversight pipeline to produce high-quality captions, and reuse the resulting critiques and preferences for post-training.","importance":"For foundation-model research, the main contribution is in supervision design rather than architecture. Video models often receive low-resolution language supervision: captions mention the main action but omit framing, camera motion, subject transitions, and spatial relations. This work treats annotation policy itself as part of the training objective. If the language interface is vague, the learned visual concepts will also be vague. The paper also offers a useful lesson for post-training: supervision need not be limited to human-written targets or binary preferences. A pipeline where the model drafts, humans critique, and the model is trained on revisions can produce richer signals: target captions, preference pairs, and natural-language error explanations. That is directly relevant to multimodal post-training and, more broadly, to how one builds high-quality data engines for foundation models.","technical_approach":"$23","experiments":"$24","reading_guide":"$25","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"如果你关心视频预训练数据质量，这篇比多数“再堆数据”工作更实在；重点看 caption schema 和 critique→preference 的监督链条，模型结果反而是次要的。","en":"If you care about video pretraining data quality, this is more useful than another scale-only paper; focus on the caption schema and the critique-to-preference supervision pipeline, not the model scores."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Carnegie Mellon University","Harvard University"],"authors":["Zhiqiu Lin","Chancharik Mitra","Siyuan Cen","Isaac Li","Yuhan Huang","Yu Tong Tiffany Ling","Hewei Wang","Irene Pi","Shihang Zhu","Ryan Rao","George Liu","Jiaxi Li","Ruojin Li","Yili Han","Yilun Du","Deva Ramanan"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["video-captioning","data-quality","human-AI-oversight","preference-data","Qwen3-VL"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21718","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.21718","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2604.21911","title":"When Prompts Override Vision: Prompt-Induced Hallucinations in LVLMs","abstract":"Despite impressive progress in capabilities of large vision-language models (LVLMs), these systems remain vulnerable to hallucinations, i.e., outputs that are not grounded in the visual input. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-23T17:54:36+00:00","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2604_21911.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI","cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在现代 LVLM 中，视觉幻觉到底主要来自哪里：视觉感知失败、语义共现先验（language prior），还是指令文本里“预设”(presupposition) 强行带入的事实？论文要把这三类来源拆开测清楚，并给出一个专门针对“指令预设诱导幻觉”的可操作缓解方案。","importance":"对基座预训练研究的直接价值不在“又一个幻觉 benchmark”，而在于把幻觉来源从“感知 vs 语言”进一步细分到“指令预设”这一更贴近对齐/后训练信号的问题：当视觉 backbone 足够强时，错误更多是跨模态对齐与指令遵循机制的副作用。对做 VLM 预训练/中后期训练的人，这提供了两个可落地的抓手：\n1) 评测侧：用可控问题族把 hallucination 归因到不同机制，而不是用单一分数混在一起；\n2) 训练侧：用偏好优化把模型从“顺着问题编”拉回到“先验证图像再回答”，本质是改写 instruction-following 的优先级。","technical_approach":"1) HalluScope 诊断集设计：围绕同一批图像构造不同“诱因”的问题族，分别测：\n- Positive Object Recognition：问图中确实存在的物体，测基本感知/识别是否可靠。\n- Random Object Recognition：问与场景无关且不存在的随机物体，测无根据的“乱答 yes”。\n- Adversarial Object Recognition：问语义上很合理、与场景共现概率高但实际不存在的物体（例如桌子旁的椅子），用来量化模型对共现先验的依赖。\n- Adversarial Presupposition Attribute：更进一步，把“物体存在”作为问题预设，再问属性（颜色/材质等），例如“椅子是什么颜色？”在图里没有椅子时会诱导模型编造一个椅子并给出颜色；这一组专门测 instruction presupposition 的支配力。\n数据构造上，作者先从 COCO 里用 caption embedding + k-center greedy 选语义覆盖更广的子集，减少评测被少数场景分布主导。\n\n2) 结论性发现（机制层面）：在不少现代 LVLM 上，感知题（Positive/Random）表现并不差，但在“共现诱导”与尤其“预设诱导”题上错误显著增多，说明主要问题不是看不见，而是语言侧（指令与先验）在冲突时压过了视觉证据。\n\n3) HalluVL-DPO 缓解：用偏好优化（DPO 类）做定向微调，目标是让“ grounded answer（承认看不到/否认预设）”在偏好上压过“顺着预设编造的回答”。关键点是 sample-informativeness：不是均匀用所有偏好对，而是更强调那些更能暴露预设诱导幻觉、梯度更有效的样本（论文表述为基于信息量的变体）。训练数据来自大规模生成的偏好对，偏好准则围绕“是否基于图像证据、是否拒绝不成立的预设”。","experiments":"全文给出的核心实验结构是：\n- 诊断：在 看主结果表：不要只看总分，按四个子集对比“感知正确但仍幻觉”的比例，这是论文最有信息量的部分。\n3) 看 HalluVL-DPO 的数据生成与偏好准则：偏好标签如何定义“grounded”，以及 sample-informativeness 的具体实现（是基于不确定性、margin、还是梯度信号）。\n4) outputs, how much is due to (i) visual perception failures, (ii) learned semantic/co-occurrence priors, or (iii) presuppositions injected by the instruction itself? It then targets the instruction-presupposition failure mode with a preference-optimization fine-tuning recipe.","importance":"The main contribution for foundation-model researchers is the reframing: as vision backbones get stronger, a large fraction of hallucinations look less like perception errors and more like a modality-alignment / instruction-following priority issue. HalluScope operationalizes this by separating co-occurrence bias from instruction presupposition, which is often conflated in prior benchmarks. On the mitigation side, the work is a concrete example of using preference optimization to reweight the model’s internal trade-off between “comply with the question” and “verify against visual evidence,” which is directly relevant to post-training signals that feed back into pretraining objectives and data design.","technical_approach":"$26","experiments":"The experimental narrative has two pillars:\n- Factorized evaluation: report per-subset performance on HalluScope across multiple LVLMs, showing the presupposition subset as the dominant failure mode and weakly correlated with pure perception errors.\n- Targeted mitigation: HalluVL-DPO reduces hallucinations on the presupposition-driven subset and yields consistent gains on established hallucination benchmarks (e.g., POPE/CHAIR/SHR/MMHAL-Bench), while aiming to preserve general multimodal capability.\n\nThe provided full-text excerpt does not include the numeric tables (model sizes, preference data scale, exact deltas). Humans resolve this tension via foveation: a coarse view guides \"where to look\", while selectively acquired high-acuity evidence refines \"what to think\". We introduce Foveated Reasoner, an autoregressive vision-language framework that unifies foveation and reasoning within a single decoding trajectory. Starting from a low-resolution view, the model triggers foveation o","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-22T20:44:24+00:00","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2604_21079.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"VLM 需要高分辨率才能看清细节，但视觉 token 数随分辨率上升导致注意力计算爆炸。现有“视觉聚焦/放大”方法要么多次跑模型（multi-pass，状态被打断、成本高），要么用文本坐标/工具调用表达注视点（token 开销大、格式脆弱）。论文要解决的是：能否把“决定看哪里”和“基于看到的证据推理”放进同一条自回归解码轨迹里，用非语言动作完成 stateful 的证据获取。","importance":"对预训练研究的相关性偏间接，但对多模态基座的训练范式有启发：\n- 把“视觉证据获取”显式建模为动作序列（policy），并与文本生成共享同一隐藏状态，这更接近可微/可学习的感知-推理闭环，而不是外部多轮调用。\n- 两阶段训练（coldstart 监督 + RL）提供了一种把离散动作（选区域、取高分辨率 crop）纳入训练的工程路径，和当前 VLM 的 tool-use / agentic perception 有接口。\n如果你的关注点是纯预训练目标与数据配比，这篇更像架构/训练流程的参考；如果做原生多模态、长上下文视觉 token 成本控制，它更实用。","technical_approach":"Foveated Reasoner（FoveateR）的核心是“单次、自回归、状态连续”的 foveation：\n1) 输入从低分辨率全局视图开始，先用少量视觉 token 建立粗理解。\n2) 在生成过程中，模型可以触发一个非语言的 foveation action（类似眼跳），选择若干区域去请求高分辨率证据。\n3) 系统从原图裁剪/提取对应高分辨率 patch，并把这些高分辨率视觉 token 注入回同一条解码轨迹（不重启模型、不重新跑一遍完整 forward），从而保持隐藏状态连续。\n\n对比已有两类方法：\n- multi-pass：每次 zoom 都要重新跑一遍 VLM，推理状态被重置；\n- text-grounded：用坐标字符串/特殊 token 表达区域，既占 token 又容易格式错。\nFoveateR 把“看哪里”从文本通道移到动作通道，减少 token 负担，也避免坐标解析脆弱性。\n\n训练：两阶段。\n- coldstart：用监督信号先教会模型基本的 foveation 行为（论文强调不需要人工标注的 foveation 轨迹，通常意味着用启发式/自动生成的区域监督或从任务信号反推）。\n- RL：再用强化学习联合优化“何时 zoom、zoom 哪、回答质量”，把证据获取策略和最终任务回报绑定。","experiments":"论文宣称在多个基准上优于近期视觉聚焦方法，并验证：\n- 在相近或更低的视觉 token/计算预算下，细粒度视觉任务准确率更高；\n- 单轨迹 stateful 机制减少多次解码 pass 的开销；\n- 相比文本坐标方案，输出更稳健（不依赖严格格式）。\n\n你给的全文片段未包含实验表格与具体数字（分辨率设置、foveation 步数、token/latency 节省、各 benchmark 提升幅度）。要做可复现的量化总结需要补齐实验章节。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（30–45 分钟）：\n1) 先看方法图（通常是 Figure 1/框架图）：确认“动作触发→取高分辨率证据→注入同一解码轨迹”的数据流，理解它为何不是 multi-pass。\n2) 精读训练部分：coldstart 的监督信号怎么来、RL 的 action space/奖励怎么定义、是否有约束避免无意义频繁 zoom。\n3) 看效率-精度曲线：同等计算预算下的提升是否来自更高分辨率 token，还是来自更好的策略。\n4) 看消融：去掉 stateful 注入、改成文本坐标、改成多 pass，性能/成本如何变化。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"High-resolution images improve VLM accuracy but explode visual-token count and attention cost. Existing visual focusing either (a) runs the model multiple times on crops (expensive and breaks reasoning continuity) or (b) encodes focus as text coordinates/tool calls (token overhead and brittle formatting). The paper aims to implement foveation as a stateful, non-linguistic action process within a single autoregressive decoding trajectory.","importance":"This is not a tokenizer/scaling-law paper, but it matters for multimodal foundation-model training as an example of making perception an explicit, learnable action policy that shares hidden state with language generation. The two-stage recipe (coldstart supervision + RL) is a practical pathway to train discrete evidence-acquisition behaviors without hand-labeled foveation trajectories, aligning with broader trends in agentic perception and tool-augmented multimodal models.","technical_approach":"Foveated Reasoner (FoveateR) integrates focusing and reasoning in one continuous AR rollout:\n1) Start from a low-res global view to keep initial visual tokens cheap.\n2) During decoding, the model can emit a foveation action (non-text) that selects regions to inspect.\n3) The system retrieves high-res evidence (crops/patches) from the original image and injects the resulting visual tokens back into the same decoding trajectory, preserving hidden-state continuity (no restart, no separate pass).\n\nThis avoids multi-pass recomputation and avoids text-based coordinate serialization.\n\nTraining is two-stage:\n- Coldstart: supervised bootstrapping of foveation behavior (the paper claims no human-annotated foveation trajectories, implying heuristic/auto-derived supervision).\n- Reinforcement learning: jointly optimize the foveation policy (when/where to zoom) and final answer quality via task reward, tying evidence acquisition to downstream success.","experiments":"The paper reports improvements over recent visual focusing baselines across multiple benchmarks, emphasizing better accuracy under constrained compute/visual-token budgets, reduced overhead versus multi-pass approaches, and robustness versus text-coordinate methods.\n\nThe provided excerpt does not include the quantitative tables (resolutions, number of foveation steps, token/latency savings, benchmark deltas). Exact numbers require the experiments section.","reading_guide":"Skim plan (30–45 min):\n1) Start with the system diagram to verify the “action → high-res retrieval → same-trajectory injection” mechanism.\n2) Read the training section carefully: action space, reward design, and how coldstart supervision is generated.\n3) Check accuracy–compute curves to see whether gains hold at matched budgets.\n4) Review ablations that replace stateful injection with multi-pass or text coordinates to isolate what actually drives improvements.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得看的是“把视觉取证动作并入解码轨迹”这个接口设计，不一定改变预训练范式，但若你在做 native multimodal 或 test-time compute，先看 RL 目标和 token-budget 曲线。","en":"What matters is the interface: visual evidence acquisition is folded into the decoding trajectory. We find that such training enables Context Unrolling, where the model explicitly reasons across multiple modal representations before producing predictions. This process enables the model to aggregate complementary information across heterogeneous modalities, facilitating a more faithful approximation of the shared multimodal knowledge manif","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-23T17:58:38+00:00","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2604_21921.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是单一模态能力不够，而是统一多模态模型在推理时如何把不同模态当作中间工作空间来逐步展开。作者把这个现象称为 Context Unrolling：模型面对一个任务时，不直接从输入到答案，而是先生成或调用若干中间模态表示，例如文本推理、视觉 token、深度、相机位姿、视角变换结果，再把这些结果写回上下文，继续约束后续预测。核心问题因此变成：怎样通过原生统一预训练，让模型学会把图像、视频、3D 几何、隐藏视觉表征和文本当作可组合的推理原语，而不是彼此隔离的任务头。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要有两层。第一，它把 native multimodal pretraining 的价值从“共享 backbone 做多任务”推进到“共享上下文做跨模态推理”，这比常见的 encoder-decoder 拼接或单向图文映射更接近统一建模。第二，它提出 hidden reasoning space 和多模态中间展开的训练思路，给多模态预训练提供了一个新的能力目标：不是只学输入输出对齐，而是学会在推理过程中显式生成有用的中间表示。就贡献量级看，这更像一个有方法雏形和系统验证的方向性工作，而不是已经完全定型的范式。对做统一多模态基座、视觉 tokenizer、AR+latent/structured reasoning 接口的人有启发，但对纯文本预训练的直接迁移还有限。","technical_approach":"方法上延续 BAGEL 的 interleaved data 思路，但把训练模态从图文扩展到文本、图像、视频、3D 几何以及隐藏视觉表征。作者的关键设计有四点。第一，训练目标不再只覆盖理解和生成，还显式加入 reasoning-oriented multimodal content，让模型在训练中接触长文本描述、密集属性、空间 grounding、深度图、相机变换等结构化中间信息。这样做的目的，是让模型在预训练阶段就见过“先展开中间表征，再做最终预测”的轨迹。第二，引入 hidden reasoning space，作为专门的潜在推理空间，用来承载不一定适合直接外显成自然语言或像素的中间状态。这个点和纯 CoT 不同，它试图给统一模型一个模态无关但可与其他模态交互的内部接口。第三，推理形式被重写为 iterative context construction：一系列 atomic primitives，例如 describe、predict pose、roll out visual tokens、synthesize novel view、estimate depth，被视为对共享上下文的读写操作。模型不是一次性解码，而是逐步扩展上下文，再在扩展后的上下文上做条件生成。第四，架构上采用 3B active parameters 的 MoE 统一模型，支持 any-to-any 学习，把理解、生成、编辑和几何预测放进同一套框架。和常见 VLM 的区别在于，后者通常把视觉输入压成单一表示后输出文本，或者把文本条件接到图像生成器上；这里强调的是中间模态可被模型自己生成、再反过来参与后续推理。","experiments":"从文中可确认的结果看，模型规模为 3B active parameters，采用 MoE。评测覆盖理解、图像生成与编辑、视频生成与编辑、以及 3D 几何。理解侧，作者称其在标准多模态理解 benchmark 上可与 Qwen3-VL、InternVL3.5 竞争，且在一个 downsampled visual understanding benchmark 上，随着 context unrolling 增加，标准 VLM 任务也能得到提升，说明中间文本推理和其他模态展开不仅服务生成，也能改善判别式问答。生成侧，在 GenEval2 上优于 Z-Image、Flux、Qwen-Image；图像编辑上定性结果强于 Step1X、Qwen-Image-Edit、Z-Image-Edit。视频侧，作者给出相对 Wan2.1 和 Hunyuan 更好的语义跟随与时序编辑定性结果。3D 几何侧，camera estimation 可比 VGGT，depth estimation 可比 Depth-Anything 3。更关键的实验信息不是单点榜单，而是作者试图证明：当模型先生成文本 CoT、视觉 token、深度或视角等中间上下文后，最终答案或生成结果更一致、更少歧义。遗憾的是，给出的全文片段里缺少完整数表和更细的消融数字，因此很难判断提升主要来自数据规模扩展、模态扩展、reasoning-oriented 数据，还是 hidden reasoning space 本身。阅读时要特别留意是否有 ablation 把这几项拆开。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓三处。第一，看 Introduction 和 Context Unrolling 定义，弄清作者到底把“统一”理解成什么；这里的重点不是多任务，而是把不同模态当作可写回上下文的中间变量。第二，看方法部分里 hidden reasoning space、atomic primitives、interleaved multimodal data 的具体实现，尤其要确认这些中间模态在训练时是监督学习、离散 token 预测，还是 latent slot。第三，看实验里关于 unrolling 步数、展开模态种类、是否显式 CoT 的消融。如果这些消融充分，这篇文章的价值主要在训练目标设计；如果不充分，则更适合作为系统案例而不是方法定论。预计 30-45 分钟可完成泛读。最值得深挖的点有两个：一是 hidden reasoning space 是否真的学到跨模态共享变量，还是只是额外容量；二是 context unrolling 的收益是否来自更长上下文和更多 test-time compute，而不是真正的统一预训练涌现。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not mainly about adding more modalities to a single model. Its central question is how a natively unified multimodal model can use different modalities as intermediate reasoning states at inference time. The authors call this Context Unrolling: instead of mapping inputs directly to outputs, the model first expands a shared workspace with intermediate textual reasoning, visual tokens, depth, camera pose, view synthesis results, or hidden representations, and then conditions later predictions on that expanded context. The real problem is therefore how to pretrain a model so that text, images, video, 3D geometry, and hidden visual representations become composable reasoning primitives rather than isolated task endpoints.","importance":"For pretraining research, the main contribution is conceptual and architectural. It shifts the value proposition of native multimodal pretraining from 'one backbone for many tasks' to 'one shared context for cross-modal reasoning.' That is a more ambitious target than standard VLM pipelines that compress vision into a single representation and decode text, or attach a text-conditioned generator on top. The paper also introduces a hidden reasoning space and argues that multimodal pretraining should teach models to generate useful intermediate representations, not just align inputs and outputs. The impact is meaningful for researchers working on unified multimodal pretraining, visual tokenization, and interfaces between autoregressive modeling and latent or structured reasoning. For pure text pretraining, the transfer is more indirect.","technical_approach":"$27","experiments":"$28","reading_guide":"For a skim, focus on three parts. First, read the Introduction and the formal description of Context Unrolling carefully. The important point is that unification is defined as shared context construction, not just multi-task parameter sharing. Second, inspect the method details around the hidden reasoning space, atomic primitives, and interleaved multimodal data. The key question is whether these intermediate modalities are explicitly supervised, tokenized, or represented as latent slots. Third, look for ablations on unrolling depth, modality choice, and explicit CoT. If those ablations are strong, the paper matters mainly as a training-objective paper; if not, it is better read as a system demonstration. A 30-45 minute skim is enough. The two deepest questions are whether the hidden reasoning space learns genuine cross-modal shared variables rather than just adding capacity, and whether the gains come from true multimodal pretraining effects or simply from longer contexts and more test-time computation.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"如果正文把 hidden representations 作为训练模态这件事讲清楚，这篇会直接影响统一多模态 pretrain 的配方；先看方法图和 ablation，别先信“会显式推理”的叙事。","en":"If the paper really makes hidden representations a first-class training modality, it could matter for unified multimodal pretraining; read the method figure and ablations first, not the “explicit reasoning” narrative."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Ceyuan Yang","Zhijie Lin","Yang Zhao","Fei Xiao","Hao He","Qi Zhao","Chaorui Deng","Kunchang Li","Zihan Ding","Yuwei Guo","Fuyun Wang","Fangqi Zhu","Xiaonan Nie","Shenhan Zhu","Shanchuan Lin","Hongsheng Li","Weilin Huang","Guang Shi","Haoqi Fan"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["native-multimodal","omni-model","context-unrolling","unified-pretraining","3d-geometry"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21921","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.21921","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2604.21277","title":"Can MLLMs \"Read\" What is Missing?","abstract":"We introduce MMTR-Bench, a benchmark designed to evaluate the intrinsic ability of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to reconstruct masked text directly from visual context. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-23T04:44:25+00:00","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2604_21277.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的问题不是“MLLM instruction following 的干扰尽量拿掉，单独测“读图时发现缺失并补全”的能力。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要在评测侧，而不是提出新的训练方法。它补上了一个过去较少被单独测量的能力维度：MLLM 是否真的学会了在原生视觉输入上做全局信息整合，而不是依赖问答模板和局部 OCR。这个维度和文档预训练、网页理解、多页长上下文建模、视觉 token 与文本 token 的对齐质量都有直接关系。量级上，它更像一个高质量诊断 benchmark，而不是改变训练范式的工作；但如果做文档 VLM、native multimodal pretrain、视觉 masking 或 layout-aware pretrain，这个 benchmark 能提供比传统 DocVQA 更干净的失效信号。","technical_approach":"方法上有三个关键设计。第一，任务定义刻意去 QA 化。输入是一页或多页图像，其中局部文本被 mask，模型直接输出缺失文本，而不是回答一个自然语言问题。这样模型必须自己完成相关区域检索、结构关联和内容恢复。第二，数据构造强调“可恢复但不靠语言先验瞎猜”。作者没有用随机 masking，而是人工挑选遮挡区域，并遵循几条约束：被遮挡内容必须和周围上下文有明确关系；剩余区域要提供足够证据；任务必须迫使模型用视觉/结构/跨区域线索，而不是只靠语言模型先验；样本要经过复核，尽量保证答案唯一。第三，评测采用 level-aware protocol。因为目标长度差异很大，短答案更适合 exact match，长答案则需要语义相似度和事实一致性判断。作者还加入了 LLM-based factuality gate，试图把自动指标和人工判断拉近，避免长文本只靠表面 n-gram 匹配失真。数据覆盖上，benchmark 包含 2771 个测试样本，含单页与多页、22 种语言、学术文档、网页截图、图表/示意图、自然场景文本等多种来源。作者还强调了时间截断，新增采集内容晚于 2025 年 6 月，以降低预训练污染风险；对已有 OCR benchmark 来源，则通过“定向遮挡后的新任务形式”来减少记忆式命中。","experiments":"实验结论很明确：这个任务对现有 MLLM 仍然偏难，尤其是句子级和段落级恢复。闭源强模型整体最好，开源小中型 VLM 落后明显。论文给出的主要观察不是某个模型小幅领先，而是能力分层很清楚：短目标、局部线索、单页样本相对容易；长目标、多页证据整合、需要事实一致性的样本显著更难。多语言和复杂版面也会进一步拉开差距。评测方法方面，作者用 level-aware 指标加 factuality gate，声称能比单一 lexical metric 更接近人工判断，这一点对长答案尤其重要。由于给出的全文片段没有完整结果表，无法可靠复述各模型的具体分数；但从文中叙述看，benchmark 的主要价值在于揭示“无提示恢复”与“有提示问答”不是同一能力，且现有模型在前者上还有明显缺口。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看任务定义和数据构造原则，重点理解作者为什么坚持去掉显式问题，以及为什么不用随机 mask。这里决定了 benchmark 是否真的在测原生视觉理解，而不是换皮 OCR。然后看 level-aware evaluation 的设计，尤其是不同长度目标为何要分层评测，以及 LLM factuality gate 如何介入。最后看结果分析部分，关注三类切分：单页 vs 多页、短答案 vs 长答案、不同来源场景。预计 20–30 分钟能抓住主要信息。如果做文档预训练或网页 recover missing text directly from visual context, without being told what to look for by an explicit question? In document and webpage settings, real inputs do not come with a natural-language query. A model must locate relevant evidence on its own, use layout and cross-region structure, and reconstruct the masked content. MMTR-Bench is designed to isolate this native recovery ability from instruction-following and prompt interpretation.","importance":"Its contribution is mainly on the evaluation side, not a new pretraining method. The benchmark probes a capability that matters for document pretraining, webpage understanding, multi-page context integration, and layout-aware multimodal modeling: whether the model has actually learned to read structured visual-text inputs globally, rather than succeeding through QA formatting or local OCR cues. The impact is diagnostic rather than paradigm-shifting, but it is a useful diagnostic. For researchers working on native multimodal pretraining, document VLMs, visual masking objectives, or long-context multimodal models, this benchmark can expose failure modes that DocVQA-style setups often blur.","technical_approach":"$29","experiments":"The main empirical message is that masked text reconstruction without explicit prompts remains difficult for current MLLMs. Strong closed-source models perform best overall, while smaller open-source vision-language models lag behind by a clear margin. Difficulty rises sharply when the target is longer, when evidence must be integrated across pages, or when factual consistency matters more than surface overlap. The benchmark also appears to separate short local completion from true global multimodal recovery. The full numeric tables are not available in the provided text fragment, so it would be unsafe to restate exact scores. Still, the qualitative pattern is clear: prompt-free reconstruction is materially harder than standard QA, and current models are far from saturated on sentence- and paragraph-level recovery.","reading_guide":"For a quick read, start with the task definition and the dataset construction rules. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-21T17:00:16+00:00","category_id":"multimodal_gen","cover_url":"/covers/2604_20902.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体但很关键的问题：在 flow matching 图像生成里，如何把“先生成低频结构，再补高频细节”这件事从网络的隐式学习动态，变成一个可控、可组合、且不破坏原始像素流路径的显式训练机制。作者认为，标准 flow matching 虽然会因为谱偏置和噪声日程自然学到一些 coarse-to-fine 顺序，但这种顺序是弱的、混杂的，频段之间存在梯度竞争，也没有明确的时间控制。已有两条路线各有代价：K-Flow 通过把频率尺度直接改写成 flow 时间来做硬约束，但会改变状态空间和轨迹定义；Latent Forcing 保留像素路径，只在辅助语义流上做异步成熟，但依赖外部预训练语义编码器，且没有直接利用图像自身的频率结构。作者要解决的，就是能否把“频率先行”作为一种 soft guidance 注入标准 flow matching，而不是重写整个生成坐标系。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要不在“图像生成指标又涨了多少”，而在建模接口设计。它把“生成顺序”拆成两个正交维度：一是按什么表示来排序，二是通过什么机制把排序注入主干生成过程。K-Flow 选的是频率轴，但机制是 hard constraint；Latent Forcing 选的是 forcing 机制，但表示是外部语义 latent。Frequency-Forcing 的价值在于把这两者重新组合：保留标准像素空间 flow matching 的路径与兼容性，用一个来自数据自身的低频 scratchpad 做异步引导。这种思路对更广义的预训练也有参考意义：很多“先学结构、后学细节”的 inductive bias，不一定要写进主目标或主状态空间，也可以通过附加流、异步时间、辅助表示来软注入。对于统一建模、多流预训练、甚至 AR/diffusion 混合范式，这是一种值得注意的接口设计。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者基本沿用了 Latent Forcing 的多时间 forcing 框架，但把辅助流从“外部语义 latent”换成“数据自带的低频表示”。主干像素分支仍然是标准线性 flow-matching 插值，不改 endpoint，不改 trajectory，因此训练和采样流程与 vanilla flow matching 保持结构一致。辅助分支则接收从图像中提取的低频 scratchpad，并通过异步时间调度让它比像素分支更早成熟，从而在去噪早期提供稳定的结构信号。这里的关键不是把低频信息直接拼接进去，而是让它作为一个先成熟的条件流，形成 soft frequency guidance。\n\n作者强调了两个设计点。第一，scratchpad 不是固定 Haar 或 Laplacian 之类的手工频率基，而是通过可学习 wavelet packet analysis filter 从数据中学出来。训练时加入 sparsity regularization 和 quadrature-mirror-filter 约束，使得到的基既稀疏又近似正交。这样做的动机很明确：固定基的低频定义更多来自设计者选择，不一定贴合数据统计；可学习基则让“什么算结构、什么算细节”由数据决定。第二，方法是 path-preserving 的。与 K-Flow 不同，它没有把 flow 时间改写成频率尺度，也没有把状态空间搬到变换域，因此可以直接兼容 vanilla flow matching pipeline，也更容易叠加别的先验。文中还专门展示了 frequency stream 和 DINO semantic stream 可以同时挂接，说明 forcing 机制在表示层面是可组合的，而不是一次只能押注一种先验。\n\n从方法论上看，这篇工作的核心创新不是提出一个全新的生成目标，而是把“频率顺序”从 transformed-space hard constraint 改成 auxiliary-stream soft guidance，并进一步把辅助表示从外部蒸馏语义改成 self-forcing 的数据内生频率表示。这种改法保住了兼容性，也降低了对大外部编码器的依赖。","experiments":"从给出的全文内容看，实验重点有三类。第一类是主结果：显式频率引导优于 plain pixel-space flow matching，说明标准 flow matching 虽然隐式存在 coarse-to-fine 动态，但强度不够；给生成路径注入低频信号后，质量有稳定增益。第二类是方法对比：作者把自己的方法放在 K-Flow 与 Latent Forcing 之间来定位。相对 K-Flow，它不追求 transformed-space 的频率编辑或 restoration 能力，而是强调在 generative quality 上用更低结构代价获得收益；相对 Latent Forcing，它不依赖 DINO 这类重型外部语义编码器，而是用轻量 wavelet packet 低频表示完成自监督式引导。第三类是消融：文中明确提到 learnable wavelet basis 优于固定 Haar 或 Laplacian basis；同时，frequency stream 之上再叠加 DINO stream 还能继续提升，说明频率先验和语义先验不是互斥关系，而是互补关系。\n\n不过，当前提供的全文片段没有包含完整实验表格、具体数据集、参数规模、FID 或其他指标的精确数字，也没有看到训练算力与采样开销的完整统计。因此能确认的实验结论是方向性的：1）soft frequency guidance 在 vanilla flow matching 之上有净增益；2）learnable basis 比固定 basis 更好；3）frequency forcing 与 semantic forcing 可叠加。若要做严格复现或判断量级，仍需查看结果章节中的主表、消融表和附录实现细节。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议先抓三处。第一，看 Introduction 里对 K-Flow 和 Latent Forcing 的拆解，重点不是背景综述，而是作者如何把“排序轴”和“注入机制”分开，这决定了整篇工作的设计空间。第二，看方法部分关于 self-forcing signal、异步时间调度、以及 learnable wavelet basis 的定义，尤其是 analysis filter 如何训练、用了哪些正则、低频 scratchpad 如何接入主干网络。第三，看实验中的三组消融：plain FM vs frequency-forcing、fixed basis vs learnable basis、frequency stream vs frequency+DINO。读这三组基本就能判断这篇工作的真实贡献在哪里。\n\n如果时间有限，预计 20 到 30 分钟可以完成一次有效泛读。优先看 Table 1 的方法定位、方法图、basis 学习公式、以及主结果与消融表。需要注意的一点是，不要把它误读成“频域 flow matching”的又一个变体；它更接近“在不改主路径的前提下，用辅助流显式安排生成顺序”。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific modeling question in flow-matching image generation: how to make coarse-to-fine generation explicit without rewriting the main flow trajectory. Standard flow matching already exhibits some low-to-high frequency ordering because low-frequency modes are easier to fit under high noise, but that ordering is weak, entangled, and not directly controllable. Existing solutions split into two camps. K-Flow enforces frequency order by redefining flow time along a frequency scale, which gives a hard constraint but changes the state space and interpolation path. Latent Forcing keeps the pixel path intact and introduces asynchronous maturation through an auxiliary semantic stream, but it relies on a heavy pretrained encoder and does not directly exploit image-native frequency structure. The paper asks whether frequency ordering can be injected as a soft guidance signal while preserving vanilla pixel-space flow matching.","importance":"Its main value is architectural rather than benchmark-driven. The paper cleanly separates two design dimensions that are often conflated: what representation should mature earlier, and by what mechanism that ordering should influence generation. K-Flow chooses frequency as the ordering axis but uses a hard transformed-space constraint. Latent Forcing chooses a flexible forcing mechanism but uses external semantic latents. Frequency-Forcing recombines these choices: it keeps the standard pixel-space flow path and uses a self-derived low-frequency scratchpad as an auxiliary stream. That is a useful pattern for pretraining research more broadly. If one wants structure-before-detail behavior, the bias does not necessarily need to be baked into the main objective or state space; it can be injected through auxiliary representations and asynchronous training dynamics. That idea is relevant beyond image generation, including multi-stream pretraining and hybrid generative paradigms.","technical_approach":"$2a","experiments":"$2b","reading_guide":"For a skim, focus on three places. First, read the introduction carefully where the paper disentangles the ordering axis from the injection mechanism. That framing is the real conceptual contribution. Second, inspect the method section for the definition of the self-forcing signal, the asynchronous time schedules, and the learnable wavelet basis, especially how the analysis filter is trained and regularized. Third, look at the core ablations: plain flow matching vs frequency-forcing, fixed basis vs learnable basis, and frequency-only vs frequency-plus-DINO.\n\nA useful skim should take about 20–30 minutes. Prioritize the method-positioning table, the architecture figure, the equations for basis learning, and the main ablation tables. One reading trap is to treat this as just another frequency-domain flow model. It is better understood as a path-preserving way to impose generation order through auxiliary streams.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"探讨 Flow Matching 中的频率引导机制，虽然是图像生成，但其对生成顺序（coarse-to-fine）的软性控制思路对连续/离散 Diffusion LM 的轨迹设计有一定启发，可扫读方法部分。","en":"Explores frequency guidance in Flow Matching. 3D 构型的 SE(3) 平移/旋转不变性）时，构造一个“真正定义在商空间上的扩散生成过程”，从而避免模型在训练/采样中学习等价类内部的无效运动（例如把整分子旋转一下），同时仍严格保证采样分布正确。","importance":"对“带对称性的生成建模”给出更原则化的处理：不是在原空间里做不变/等变然后让网络自己学会忽略冗余自由度，而是把扩散过程本身改写为商空间上的随机过程，再通过可实现的 lifted 过程落回原空间执行。对预训练研究的直接关联不强（偏科学计算/几何生成），但对 diffusion LM / 连续空间扩散的训练动态有启发：当目标分布存在等价类时，去掉等价类方向的噪声与更新，可以降低学习难度、减少无意义的梯度信号，并缓解“训练目标与采样过程不一致”的对齐式启发法问题。","technical_approach":"1) 形式化：给定群 G 在空间 X 上的作用，目标分布实际定义在商空间 X/G（每个等价类视为一个点）。作者从 Wiener process 在原空间与商空间的对应关系出发，推导商空间上的扩散/随机插值过程。\n2) 可实现性问题：商空间通常非欧、难以直接数值模拟。作者用 differential geometry 里的 horizontal lift，把商空间上的随机过程“提升”到原空间 X 上，得到一个可在欧式坐标中实现的 SDE/ODE 形式。\n3) 关键操作：在原空间的每一步更新向量上做投影，去掉会导致等价类内部移动的分量，仅保留“横向（horizontal）”方向的更新。直观上：不再让模型输出包含整体旋转/平移的速度场，而只学习真正改变内禀状态（如分子形状）的部分。\n4) 与现有方法差异：\n- 相比数据增强或等变网络：它们仍在原空间推进扩散，网络会被迫在等价类方向上给出某种具体运动；这里直接把动力学约束到横向子空间。\n- 相比 alignment（对齐到参考构型）式训练：alignment 改了监督目标但采样动力学仍在原空间，容易出现训练-采样不一致；本文把采样过程也一并改成与“去冗余自由度”一致的 lifted 过程。","experiments":"论文把方法落到 directly on a quotient space induced by symmetries (e.g., SE(3) for molecular 3D structures), so that the model does not waste capacity learning arbitrary motions within an equivalence class (global rotation/translation), while still guaranteeing the correct target distribution during sampling.","importance":"This is a principled alternative to the common “stay in the ambient space and enforce invariance/equivariance” recipe. By modifying the diffusion dynamics itself to live on the quotient space (and implementing it via a lifted process), the method removes redundant degrees of freedom from both training targets and sampling trajectories. It is not a core LLM-pretraining paper, but it is relevant to diffusion-style language modeling and continuous diffusion training dynamics: when the data distribution has symmetry-induced equivalence classes, projecting updates away from the orbit directions can reduce gradient noise and mitigate train–sample mismatch seen in heuristic alignment-based approaches.","technical_approach":"1) Formal setup: a group G acts on a space X, and the true generative distribution is effectively on the quotient X/G.\n2) Quotient diffusion: derive a diffusion / stochastic interpolant process on X/G by relating Brownian motions on X and on the quotient.\n3) Practical simulation via geometry: since X/G is typically non-Euclidean, use the horizontal lift construction to induce an equivalent process back on X.\n4) Operational consequence: at each step, project the ambient-space update/velocity onto the horizontal subspace (orthogonal to the group-orbit directions). This removes components that only move samples within the same equivalence class.\n5) Contrast to prior work:\n- Data augmentation / equivariant networks still evolve in X, so the model implicitly learns some arbitrary orbit motion.\n- Alignment heuristics change the supervision target but keep sampling dynamics in X, creating inconsistencies; here the sampling process is modified consistently with the quotient formulation.","experiments":"Applied to molecular structure generation (R^{3N}/SE(3)) and protein backbone design.\n- Small molecules (GEOM-QM9, GEOM-DRUGS): reported ~9%–23% relative improvements over ET-Flow, and better than heuristic alignment baselines.\n- Protein backbone generation: with the same parameter scale (60M), substantially better than Proteína on key distributional metrics; Speech","abstract":"Simultaneous speech translation (SST) generates translations while receiving partial speech input. Recent advances show that large language models (LLMs) can substantially improve SST quality, but at the cost of high computational overhead. To reduce this cost, prior work reformulates SST as a multi-turn dialogue task, enabling full reuse of the LLM's key-value (KV) cache and eliminating redundant feature recomputation. However, this approach relies on supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data in dialog","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-22T19:43:51+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2604_21045.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是流式同声传译里的一个很具体但很棘手的问题：如何在长时、无界语音输入下，同时兼顾翻译质量、时延和推理效率。已有路线把 SST 改写成多轮对话式的 read–write 生成，从而复用语音编码器和 LLM 的 KV cache，推理上很省，但训练依赖合成的交错轨迹数据。这类轨迹往往带噪声：词对齐法不理解未来上下文，LLM 分段法又不保证 read/write 决策合法。结果是模型会学到错误的等待或抢跑行为。论文的核心问题不是再造一个新架构，而是如何在 imperfect SFT 之后，用一个稳定的后训练方法把策略纠偏，而且不能让“低时延”这个容易优化的目标压过“翻译正确”这个更难目标。","importance":"对基座研究的直接价值不在 SST 任务本身，而在训练信号设计。它给了一个很清楚的例子：当多目标优化里不同 reward 的难度和梯度形态差很多时，简单加权和往往会把策略推向错误方向。这里 latency reward 很容易被模型投机性利用，只要更早输出就能拿分，但质量会掉。作者用层级式 reward 把“先过质量门槛，再谈时延”写进优化目标，这比调一个静态权重更接近真实偏好约束。这个思路对 RLHF、推理模型 post-training、以及任何 accuracy–cost / quality–latency 联合优化问题都有参考意义。贡献量级上，它更像一个扎实的训练方法改进，而不是范式变化；但在“如何让 RL 修正有噪 SFT 数据”这件事上，信息密度是够的。","technical_approach":"方法建立在 InfiniSST 架构上：前端是流式语音编码器，按 chunk 增量编码并复用历史 KV；后端是 LLM 翻译器，输入是交错的语音特征和已生成文本，遇到 <EOT> 就结束当前 chunk 的输出。为了支持无界长语音，语音侧用 sliding window，LLM 侧配 Attention Sink。训练上先用合成的 interleaving 数据做 SFT，再做 HPO 后训练。\n\nHPO 的主体是对 GRPO 的改造。对每个长语音片段，策略采样多个候选翻译轨迹，分别计算质量奖励和时延奖励。关键不在“用了 GRPO”，而在 reward 结构：\n1. 质量奖励和时延奖励分开计算，而不是先线性合并。\n2. 设一个质量阈值；如果某条轨迹的质量没过线，则其 latency reward 直接打到最小值。这样模型不能靠提前输出换取总 reward。\n3. 对质量和时延 reward 分别做 group normalization，再合并成最终优势信号，减少某一项 reward 方差或尺度主导训练。\n\n这个设计针对的是 SST 的结构性难点。时延目标天然单调，容易学成“尽早说”；质量目标则稀疏、延迟、且受整句一致性影响。层级 reward 相当于把优化从“软权衡”改成“带门控的偏好约束”。从全文表述看，作者还强调这是在有噪轨迹监督上的纠偏，而不是从零学 read/write policy，因此它依赖一个还算可用的 SFT 初始化。论文也说明了训练样本构造：从分钟到小时级长录音中切出最长 67.2 秒的片段，每段 60 个 chunk、每个 chunk 1.12 秒；先用 ASR 时间戳对齐语音和转写，再用 SimAlign 做 transcript–translation 对齐，生成交错格式数据。","experiments":"实验主要在 ACL 60/60 dev 和 RealSI 上做，关注的是不同平均时延下的翻译质量。摘要里给出的代表性结果是：在平均时延 1.5 秒时，相比最强基线，HPO 带来 +7 COMET、+1.25 MetricX、+4 BLEURT 的提升。这说明它不是只在某个单一指标上讨巧，而是在多种质量评测上都改善。\n\n从全文设定看，实验重点应包括三类比较：\n1. 与仅 SFT 的 InfiniSST 比较，验证后训练是否能修正合成轨迹噪声带来的策略偏差。\n2. 与直接把质量和时延做平权或简单加权的 RL 目标比较，验证层级 reward 的必要性。\n3. 与不同 latency operating points 的比较，证明改进不是只出现在高时延或低时延某一端，而是在较宽时延区间都成立。\n\n论文最有价值的消融通常在 reward 设计上：去掉质量阈值门控后，模型会更倾向于抢跑；不做分项 group normalization 时，训练更不稳，或者某一 reward 主导更新。由于给出的全文片段没有完整表格，无法逐项复述所有数字，但主结论很明确：HPO 在固定时延下提升质量，在质量相近时也能压低时延，而且这种收益出现在长时流式场景而不是短句离线翻译。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓三处。\n\n第一，看 Introduction 和 Figure 1，弄清作者为什么坚持 dialogue-style SST。重点不是任务背景，而是 KV cache 复用如何把长时流式翻译做成可扩展的生成问题。\n\n第二，看 HPO 方法部分和 Figure 2。这里要重点理解两件事：为什么 latency reward 比 quality reward 更容易被优化；为什么“质量不过线则时延记最差”比简单加权更合理。若只看公式而不看动机，很容易把它误读成普通 reward shaping。\n\n第三，看实验里的 reward ablation 和 latency-quality tradeoff 图。这里最能看出方法是否真的在纠偏，而不是单纯把 operating point 往高时延方向推。\n\n如果只是判断是否值得跟进，30 分钟足够：读引言、方法图、主结果表和 reward 消融。若关心 RL for noisy supervision，可再细看训练细节、阈值设定、group normalization 的实现位置，以及不同评测指标之间是否一致。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles a specific but important failure mode in simultaneous speech translation under long, effectively unbounded audio streams: how to improve translation quality and latency jointly when the model is trained on noisy synthesized read/write trajectories. Recent SST systems reformulate the task as an interleaved dialogue so they can fully reuse KV cache across speech and text, which is attractive for long-context streaming inference. The problem is that the supervision used to train those read/write behaviors is imperfect. Alignment-based synthesis ignores future context needed for proper timing, while LLM-based segmentation does not guarantee valid trajectories. As a result, the model learns brittle waiting and premature-writing behaviors. The paper asks how to correct those behaviors in post-training without letting the easy-to-optimize latency objective dominate the harder quality objective.","importance":"Its value for foundation-model researchers is less about SST as an application and more about multi-objective RL under uneven reward difficulty. The paper gives a clean case where a naive weighted sum is structurally misaligned with the desired behavior: latency reward is easy to game, because emitting earlier often improves the score even when translation quality degrades. The proposed hierarchical reward encodes a preference constraint directly: quality must clear a threshold before latency can matter. That idea is relevant beyond speech translation, especially for RLHF-style settings, reasoning-model post-training, and any quality-cost or quality-latency optimization where one reward is much easier to exploit than another. This is not a new modeling paradigm, but it is a solid training-design contribution on how RL can repair noisy SFT supervision.","technical_approach":"$2c","experiments":"$2d","reading_guide":"$2e","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得看奖励设计：把“延迟”做成可优化的层级信号而不是硬约束；只读奖励定义与消融，别被同传应用细节带跑偏。","en":"Read for the reward design: latency as an optimizable hierarchical signal rather than a hard constraint; focus on reward/ablations, not the SST application details."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Carnegie Mellon University","NVIDIA"],"authors":["Siqi Ouyang","Shuoyang Ding","Oleksii Hrinchuk","Vitaly Lavrukhin","Brian Yan","Boris Ginsburg","Lei Li"],"notable_authors":["Boris Ginsburg","Lei Li"],"tags":["rl-post-training","reward-design","latency-quality-tradeoff","simultaneous-translation","kv-cache-reuse"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21045","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.21045","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2604.21593","title":"Language as a Latent Variable for Reasoning Optimization","abstract":"As LLMs reduce English-centric bias, a surprising trend emerges: non-English responses sometimes outperform English on reasoning tasks. Instead of relying on English-centric preference data or test-time multilingual voting, it builds online preference groups from same-question, different-language trajectories, which can improve credit assignment and exploration with rule-based rewards.","technical_approach":"polyGRPO = multilingual trajectory sampling + group-relative policy optimization.\n- Polyglot Thinking Experiment: for each problem, compare (i) constrained response language vs (ii) unconstrained response language. Findings: Chinese can beat English on average under constraints; no single language dominates; unconstrained often beats English-only.\n- Training group construction: for each question, sample one unconstrained response and several constrained responses in randomly chosen target languages, forming a group of diverse reasoning paths.\n- Reward: rule-based, combining final-answer correctness and formatting/step-structure incentives.\n- Optimization: apply GRPO (no value function) with group-relative advantages computed within each group; update the policy toward higher-reward trajectories. Through an empirical study, we observe that responses with medium consistency form an ambiguity region and constitute the primary source of reward noise. Crucially, we find that such spurious signals can be even amplified through group-relative advantage estimation. Motivated by these findings, we propose a unified framework,","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-23T06:32:08+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2604_21327.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是 test-time reinforcement learning 在无标注数学推理场景里的一个核心失真：模型用自己采样出来的答案做多数投票，再把这个伪标签当奖励信号去更新自己，结果奖励里混入了大量噪声，而且这种噪声会被 GRPO 的 group-relative advantage 进一步放大。作者要解决的不是一般意义上的 RL 稳定性，而是 TTRL 这个特定闭环里“伪标签不可靠 + 优势归一化放大偏差”带来的错误学习信号。","importance":"它对预训练本身不是直接贡献，但对 pretrain ↔ post-train 接口有现实意义。原因有两点。第一，越来越多工作在探索 test-time adaptation、RLVR、self-improvement 这类不依赖人工标注的强化学习路径，这些方法常被视为后训练阶段的低成本增益手段，也可能反过来影响 continual pretrain 或 mid-train 策略。第二，这篇工作把一个常被经验性描述的问题拆成了可分析的两部分：伪标签噪声主要集中在哪些样本上，以及优化器/优势估计如何系统性放大这种噪声。这个分析框架对任何“模型生成监督再训练模型”的闭环都有参考价值，不只限于数学 TTRL。就相关性而言，它更偏 post-train / reasoning adaptation，而不是核心预训练论文，所以 skim 合适。","technical_approach":"作者的方法叫 DDRL，包含三步，对应三类噪声来源。\n\n第一步是 balanced confidence-aware sampling。作者先分析 repeated sampling 后答案频次与正确率的关系，发现高频答案大多可靠，低频答案大多错误，而中等频次处于 ambiguity region，正确率波动大，是伪奖励噪声的主要来源。因此他们不再像原始 TTRL 那样把所有 rollout 一视同仁，而是基于频次筛掉中频、保留更可靠的高频正样本和低频负样本，同时维持正负样本平衡。这里的关键不是简单“只保留高置信样本”，因为 RL 还需要有区分度的正负对比；所以作者强调 balanced sampling，而不是纯过滤。\n\n第二步是 debiased advantage estimation。原始 TTRL 使用 GRPO，在每个 group 内对 reward 做相对归一化。问题在于，当伪标签本身有噪声、而 group 共识又不高时，这种相对归一化会把少量错误奖励放大成更大的 advantage，导致模型朝错误方向更新。作者给出理论分析说明这种偏差在低共识区更严重。为此，DDRL 用固定的、标签依赖的 advantage 取代 group-relative normalization，目标是消除由组内统计量引入的额外偏差。直观上，这相当于承认“奖励已经不干净”，因此不再让组内均值和方差进一步扭曲它。\n\n第三步是 consensus-based off-policy refinement。RL 阶段结束后，作者再构造一个基于共识的 rejection sampling 数据集，做轻量的离策略精修。这个阶段更像把 RL 过程中积累的较高置信样本转成稳定的监督信号，用更低方差的方式巩固收益。为什么不一开始就只做 rejection sampling？因为 TTRL 的目标是 test-time 适配，先用 RL 在当前测试分布上推动策略移动，再用离策略 refinement 稳定收尾，兼顾了适应性和稳定性。","experiments":"摘要给出的主结果是：相对原始 TTRL，DDRL 在 Qwen2.5-MATH-1.5B 上带来 15.3% 的相对提升，在 LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct 上带来 12.7% 的相对提升，并且在多个数学 benchmark 上都有效。这里最值得看的不是单个分数，而是作者是否把提升拆解到三个组件上。\n\n从全文引言和方法部分看，关键实验应包括：一，答案频次与正确率的关系曲线，证明中频样本确实是 ambiguity region；二，GRPO advantage 在不同共识水平下的偏差分析，最好同时有理论推导和实证可视化；三，balanced sampling、debiased advantage、off-policy refinement 三个模块的逐步消融，确认收益不是只来自最后的 rejection sampling；四，不同模型规模和不同数学数据集上的泛化，说明这不是某个模型的偶然现象。\n\n如果论文还报告了 rollout 数 N、采样温度、测试时更新步数、以及不同 pseudo-label 纯度下的性能变化，这些会很有价值，因为它们直接决定 TTRL 的成本和稳定性边界。需要保留一点谨慎：这类方法的收益通常依赖任务可验证性、答案离散度和多数投票有效性。数学推理适合做这件事，不代表开放式生成任务也能照搬。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Figure 1 和 Figure 2 一类的核心图。前者通常说明 TTRL 到 DDRL 的流程差异，后者是整篇论文最重要的经验发现：答案频次与正确率并不是单调线性可用，而是中频区域最危险。然后看理论部分里关于 GRPO advantage bias 的推导，重点不是公式细节，而是弄清楚为什么 group-relative normalization 在伪标签噪声下会放大错误信号。最后看 ablation table，确认三步设计各自贡献多少。\n\n如果只想判断这篇论文是否值得跟进，读 Introduction、问题分析图、debiased advantage 那一节、主结果表和消融，30 分钟左右足够。若要更深入，可以看附录里的 Bayesian 解释和更多 rollout 统计，因为这些内容决定“中频歧义区”是不是一个稳健现象。真正值得带走的点有两个：一是 self-generated supervision 的噪声往往集中在不确定边界样本，而不是均匀分布；二是相对优势归一化在无监督伪奖励场景下未必是中性的，它可能主动放大坏信号。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a specific failure mode of test-time reinforcement learning for math reasoning. In TTRL, the model samples multiple answers, derives a pseudo-label by majority vote, and then updates itself using that pseudo-label as reward supervision. This creates a closed loop where reward noise is unavoidable. The paper argues that the problem is not just noisy pseudo-labels in general, but the combination of noisy pseudo-labels and GRPO-style group-relative advantage normalization, which can systematically amplify wrong learning signals.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, but it is relevant to the pretrain-posttrain interface. Test-time adaptation, RL with verifiable rewards, and self-improvement loops are increasingly used as low-label post-training tools, and the lessons often transfer to continual pretraining or mid-training settings where model-generated supervision is reused. The paper is useful because it decomposes the issue into two analyzable parts: where pseudo-label noise is concentrated, and how the optimization procedure magnifies it. That framing is broader than math TTRL. It applies to many self-training pipelines where the model generates supervision for itself.","technical_approach":"$2f","experiments":"$30","reading_guide":"$31","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"如果你在看 R1-Zero、test-time adaptation 或自举式 RL，这篇值得读噪声分析部分；方法未必会留下，但“中等一致性样本+相对优势放大偏差”这个诊断应当复现。","en":"If you care about R1-Zero-style self-bootstrapped RL or test-time adaptation, read this for the noise diagnosis; the method may not stick, but the medium-consistency plus relative-advantage amplification story is worth reproducing."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Institute of Automation, Chinese Academy of Sciences","University of Chinese Academy of Sciences","Meituan","NLPR & MAIS, Institute of Automation, Chinese Academy of Sciences"],"authors":["Yongcan Yu","Lingxiao He","Jian Liang","Kuangpu Guo","Meng Wang","Qianlong Xie","Xingxing Wang","Ran He"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["test-time-rl","math-reasoning","reward-noise","advantage-estimation","pseudo-labeling"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21327","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.21327","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2604.21495","title":"Generalizing Numerical Reasoning in Table Data through Operation Sketches and Self-Supervised Learning","abstract":"Numerical reasoning over expert-domain tables often exhibits high in-domain accuracy but limited robustness to domain shift. 直接生成问答或程序时容易出现语义与程序不一致；三是对 header 的词面依赖过强，换个命名方式就掉点明显。作者要解决的不是单一 benchmark continual pretraining 配方：用结构草图提供低熵推理支架，用实例级匿名化打断词面记忆，再用 program-first 自监督扩充高一致性训练信号。这个思路不只适用于表格 QA，也适用于代码执行、工具调用、形式化推理等“答案可验证、结构可抽象”的场景。贡献量级不在于提出全新模型架构，而在于把数据构造、输入表示和中间训练目标组合成一个能显著改善跨域泛化的数据-目标设计。","technical_approach":"$32","experiments":"$33","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读到中度精读，重点不在公式，而在问题拆解和数据构造。\n\n建议先看 header-shift 设置。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific failure mode in table numerical reasoning: models fine-tuned on a domain dataset often achieve decent in-domain accuracy but fail badly under schema or terminology shift. The authors argue that standard SFT encourages shortcut learning, especially brittle header-to-operation associations, instead of stable structural reasoning over tables. From the full paper, the problem is decomposed into three failure modes: wasted capacity on trivial arithmetic pattern recovery, scarcity of logically consistent supervision, and strong lexical dependence on headers. The goal is not merely to improve one benchmark, but to make numerical reasoning transfer across related table domains by separating domain wording from computational structure.","importance":"For pretraining-oriented readers, the value is in how the paper reframes a downstream robustness issue as a data/objective design problem. The work shows that when lexical cues, table schema, and operation templates are entangled in supervision, the model learns the cheapest correlations first. That is a familiar pattern in pretraining: shortcut absorption, surface-form overfitting, and weak structural generalization. TaNOS offers a practical continual-pretraining recipe built around three levers: explicit structural scaffolds, lexical de-correlation, and correctness-controlled synthetic supervision. The contribution is not a new backbone, but a clean example of how intermediate training can reshape what the model allocates capacity to. The ideas are relevant beyond table QA, especially for executable reasoning tasks such as code, tool use, and formal or semi-formal reasoning where latent structure can be exposed and outputs can be verified.","technical_approach":"$34","experiments":"$35","reading_guide":"$36","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这类“去语义化 + 程序优先自监督”配方对基座研究有真信息量；先看消融和跨域表，若 setup 干净，值得内部复现到 math/table mid-training。","en":"This de-semanticization plus program-first self-supervision recipe carries real signal for foundation work; check the ablations and cross-domain tables first, and replicate internally for math/table mid-training if the setup is clean."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Seoul National University","Soongsil University"],"authors":["Hanjun Cho","Gahyun Yoo","Hanseong Kim","Jay-Yoon Lee"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["continual-pretraining","numerical-reasoning","table-qa","self-supervision","shortcut-learning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21495","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.21495","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2604.20904","title":"Reinforcing privacy reasoning in LLMs via normative simulacra from fiction","abstract":"Information handling practices of LLM agents are broadly misaligned with the contextual privacy expectations of their users. We propose extracting normative simulacra (structured representations of norms and infor","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-21T19:16:22+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2604_20904.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的问题很直接：如何让 LLM 在涉及隐私的信息流决策中，学会按情境规范进行推理，而不是只做表面上的“少收集数据”或依赖推理时外挂审查器。作者采用 Contextual Integrity（CI）作为隐私框架，把隐私理解为“信息在特定社会情境中的适当流动”，然后尝试在训练阶段把这种规范性推理能力写进模型。核心难点不在于再造一个 privacy benchmark，而在于训练数据从哪里来：现实世界里带有完整社会语境、角色关系、信息属性和传递原则的高质量隐私推理样本很稀缺。作者的回答是，从小说中抽取规范宇宙，用结构化的 normative simulacra 作为监督和强化学习信号。","importance":"从基座预训练视角看，这篇工作不是预训练方法论文，而是一个较有意思的 post-train 数据与奖励设计案例。它的启发点有两个。第一，作者把“虚构文本中的社会规范”转成结构化训练信号，这和 textbook-style synthetic supervision 有相通之处：不是直接追求任务标签，而是先构造一个可供模型内化的 reasoning substrate。第二，它把 RL 奖励从单纯格式正确或偏好对齐，推进到“是否与一个显式规范宇宙一致”。这对更广义的对齐训练有参考意义：如果目标能力涉及规则、制度、伦理或多方关系，仅靠通用偏好数据往往不够，显式世界模型或规范库可能是更稳的奖励锚点。不过，这种价值主要停留在特定能力训练接口上，对预训练本身的直接影响有限。","technical_approach":"方法分三步。第一步是构建 normative simulacra。作者从 10 本 Project Gutenberg 公版小说中抽取两类结构化对象：一类是 CI information flow tuples，描述谁把什么信息传给谁、在什么情境和传递原则下发生；另一类是与 Raz-anatomy norms 对应的规范表示，用来刻画文本内部的社会规则与允许/禁止关系。作者把这些结构化对象视为每部小说的“规范宇宙”。选择小说而不是现实合规语料，原因是小说往往有完整社会结构、稳定角色关系和大量规范冲突场景，更适合教模型学会情境化判断。\n\n第二步是两阶段训练。先做 SFT，让模型学习输出带有 CI 结构的隐私推理。这里的目标不是简单分类，而是让模型显式写出信息流、相关规范和判断依据。再做 GRPO 强化学习，用一个组合奖励来约束输出质量。根据文中描述，奖励包含程序化信号，例如任务清晰度、结构完整性，以及一个更关键的 normative grounding 信号：由 LLM judge 在可访问源文本 held-out norms 的条件下，判断模型输出是否与该文本的规范宇宙一致。这样做的意图，是让模型不仅“会说隐私术语”，而是真的围绕某个规范系统做推理。\n\n第三步是评测。作者在五个 CI-aligned benchmark 上测试迁移效果，想回答两个问题：从小说抽出的规范推理能否迁移到现实语境；以及 RL 是否能在 SFT 之上进一步提升规范一致性。方法上的新意不在模型架构，而在数据构造和 reward grounding：训练信号来自 fiction-derived structured norms，强化学习的 judge 也不是泛泛偏好打分，而是参考一个显式规范库。","experiments":"从全文片段可以确认的实验设计是：使用 10 本小说构建训练语料，在 5 个与 Contextual Integrity 对齐的 benchmark 上评估，并做 SFT 与 SFT+GRPO 的对比及相关消融。作者的主张是两点：一，来自 fiction 的 normative simulacra 能教会模型一定的 contextual privacy reasoning，并迁移到真实场景评测；二，带有规范 grounding 的 GRPO 比单纯 SFT 更能提升结构完整性和规范一致性。\n\n但当前给出的全文内容没有包含结果表、基座模型名称与参数规模、训练样本量、GRPO 轮数、奖励权重、以及各 benchmark 上的具体分数变化。因此还不能严谨地判断效果量级，也无法确认提升是来自 fiction 数据本身、CI 结构化标注、还是 RL reward 设计。若要判断这篇工作的可信度，实验部分需要重点核对三件事：1）是否有强基线，例如直接用通用 instruction tuning 或现实隐私数据微调；2）是否有跨小说、跨情境的 held-out 评测，避免模型只记住特定文本规范；3）LLM judge 的偏置是否被控制，例如是否有人类评审或程序化一致性校验作补充。","reading_guide":"泛读建议很明确。先看方法部分里 normative simulacra 的抽取流程，确认结构化对象到底是什么、自动化程度多高、人工校验占比多少。再看 GRPO 奖励设计，尤其是 composite reward 的各项组成，以及 judge 如何访问 held-out norms。最后看实验章节中的 grounding，而不在模型或优化器层面。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a concrete alignment problem: how to train LLMs to reason about privacy as context-dependent information It adopts Contextual Integrity (CI) as the target framework, where privacy depends on whether information flows are appropriate for a given social context. The hard part is not defining the principle but obtaining training data rich enough to teach it. Real-world privacy datasets rarely contain complete social context, actor relations, transmission principles, and explicit normative reasoning. The paper’s answer is to extract structured “normative simulacra” from fiction and use them as supervision and RL grounding.","importance":"For a foundation-model researcher, this is not a pretraining paper, but it is a useful post-training case study in data construction and reward design. Second, its RL stage is grounded not just in format or generic preference signals, but in consistency with an explicit normative universe. That is relevant beyond privacy. Whenever the target capability involves rules, institutions, ethics, or multi-party constraints, generic preference tuning may be too weak, and explicit world models or norm libraries may provide a better reward anchor.
